{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"galls","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A sore on a horse caused by an ill-fitted or ill-adjusted saddle; a saddle sore."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A sore or open wound caused by chafing, which may become infected, as with a blister."],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["medicine","pathology","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A feeling of exasperation."],"tags":["figuratively","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A pit on a surface being cut caused by the friction between the two surfaces exceeding the bond of the material at a point."],"tags":["countable"],"topics":["engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","technical"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ɡɔːl/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ɡoːl/","tags":["Australia","British","New-Zealand","Southern","Standard"]},{"ipa":"/ɡɔl/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/ɡɑl/","tags":["US","cot-caught-merger"]},{"ipa":"/ɡɔl/","tags":["Scotland"]}],"upstream_line":230037,"upstream_offset":1130398751,"upstream_record_sha256":"55720974358947ce69a5a8a7dc8d9bdc5ce228e41c4a83d9c19b31d68feba342","word":"gall"}
